## 5. Tests (named cases)

`packages/lineage/test/locality-power.test.ts`
- `PANEL_V1 reproduces PR136 neighbourhood, draw and protocol byte for byte`
- `power panel rule thresholds, ASSAY values except seeds, tau formula and gate equal PR136's`
- `power seeds and salt are disjoint from PR136's and no assayed hash is shared with PR136 rows`
- `disjointness test fails for a panel built with PR136's seeds` (negative fixture)
- `every stratum population covers its allocation for c4 and c9 (or the frozen deficit rule applies)`
- `allocation sums to 192 per M3 genome and 8 for the control`
- `power computation: P(A>=5) at p = 1/24 for N = 192 equals the amendment's 0.905 (exact rational)`
- `decide: A = 4 is inconclusive, A = 5 on both genomes is decidable, regardless of N` and `no-signal panel is inconclusive, not a null verdict`
- `fixtures stay neutral/non-neutral as before under the new seeds` (slow, Deno-free subset)

`tools/test/locality-power-evidence.test.ts` (Deno subprocess for slow parts, as PR136)
- `freeze.json seal verifies and its chosen set matches a recomputation without assays`
- `evidence --check passes; fails on: edited row, edited verdict, edited seed list, missing freeze, freeze mismatch, resealed derived field`
- `write refuses without freeze, with a tampered freeze, or if evidence exists`
- `worker pool result equals serial result on a small subset (1 vs 2 workers)`
- `--replay-check passes` (full)

`apps/lab/test`
- `page renders both panels, selector switches data, side-by-side table matches the two payload verdicts`
- `packaging: prototype still private and outside the deployed site`
- PR136 `tools/test/locality-evidence.test.ts` and `packages/lineage/test/locality.test.ts` remain green unchanged.

## 6. Order of work and acceptance gates

1. Panel refactor; gate: `deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/matrix.ts --replay-check` ok (PR136 byte-identical), then re-pin manifest sources and `--check` ok.
2. Write AMENDMENT.md first, containing both exact allocations (N = 192 and the fallback N = 144, each with its deficit-rule output and stated probabilities), the timing workload and the selection rule. Timing is measured on a baseline-only workload (start-genome baseline assays per genome under both seed pairs; `power.ts --calibrate` has no code path that assays a mutant, and a test asserts that): median of 3 serial runs times the per-edit multiplier. Rule: N = 192 if projected <= 20 min loaded, else N = 144 if projected <= 20 min, else stop and report without running (no third N). Timing is outcome-free, so choosing N from it is allowed; the choice is recorded in `freeze.json` and the amendment is dated before the freeze commit.
3. Run `power.ts --freeze` (preflights included: pool sizes, c4/c9 breadth reachability, gate/tau); Sol xhigh review of the amendment/freeze; fixes; commit only AMENDMENT.md, PLAN.md, freeze.json, the freeze code and tests, push `claude/i05-power`.
4. Run once: `deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/power.ts --write --workers 2`, then `--check` and `--replay-check`; mechanical verdict; RESULT.md.
5. Page and tests; typecheck, full suite.

Runnable gates:
```
export PATH=~/.nvm/versions/node/v24.2.0/bin:$PATH
pnpm install
pnpm typecheck
pnpm vitest run --maxWorkers=2 packages/lineage/test/locality.test.ts packages/lineage/test/locality-power.test.ts tools/test/locality-evidence.test.ts tools/test/locality-power-evidence.test.ts apps/lab/test
pnpm test --maxWorkers=2
deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/matrix.ts --check && deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/matrix.ts --replay-check
deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/power.ts --check && deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/power.ts --replay-check --workers 1 && deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/power.ts --replay-check --workers 2   # subprocess deadlines 2x projected run; vitest wrappers use explicit timeouts, not the 60 s default or PR136's 600 s
git diff --exit-code e56077bd -- packages/schema packages/sim-ref packages/sim-gpu experiments/preregistration.md experiments/FROZEN docs/rules.md   # must exit 0 (PR136's protected set)
pnpm vitest run packages/sim-ref/test/golden-hashes.test.ts   # pins untouched
```
Then page check: `pnpm dev`, open `/controller-locality/`, both panels, screenshot at 375 and 1280 px.

## 7. Non-goals and limits

- No modular controller, no search/evolution run, no new scenarios, no new mutation operator, no change to the decision rule, tau, gate or thresholds, no GPU, no paid calls, no deploy, no change to the frozen preregistration.
- Not pooled with PR136; no claim about evolved creatures in general. Biomass responses are not independent capabilities (as in PR136). Short horizon (200), tiny tile, two seeds.
- N = 192 is a power choice under the observed rate; a low true rate (about 2%) makes a second inconclusive likely (P(A >= 5) 0.34), and that is reported as such. Even with A >= 5, the go/no-go for a fork is advisory only; any controller-layout change needs its own versioned plan, migration and CPU/WGSL parity.
- Wall time depends on machine load; timing is provenance, not part of the deterministic payload.
- Entanglement on c4 is structurally unreachable (see 2.2); a RESULT of `inconclusive` is not evidence about entanglement either way.
- Optional-stopping, extra runs after seeing A, or re-drawing the assayed set are prohibited; a reviewer-found defect before the run restarts from the freeze commit with a new dated amendment.

## 8. Revision note

Revised after the Sol 6.1 xhigh plan review (raw: `/tmp/controller-locality-power-2026-11-02-plan-review.out.txt`, final message `/tmp/i05p/plan-review-final.txt`). Dispositions in `REVIEW.md`, "Plan review dispositions".
# Amendment 1 (2026-11-02): I05b power-up of the controller-locality assay

Status: written and committed BEFORE any edited genome of this panel was assayed, and before `freeze.json` existed (the freeze binds the SHA-256 of this file). Nothing below was chosen after seeing a mutant outcome of the new panel; the only outcome-like numbers used are PR136's, which are already public (`docs/controller-locality-2026-11-01/`). Exploratory, CPU only, no GPU evidence. Local, off the deployed site.

Scope as given by the coordinator (task text; decision D12 is in `docs/backlog-run-2026-11-01.html`, D14 is quoted from the task text and is not in that file): I05 (PR136) ended inconclusive (underpowered): only 1 of 24 assayed edits improved a response for each M3 genome, against the pre-stated minimum of 5 improving edits (`RULE.minImproving`), and its EVIDENCE.md says assayed edits stayed at 24 per genome though runtime allowed more. This slice re-asks the same question with enough assayed edits for the unchanged rule to be able to decide.

## 1. What does not change

- Genomes: M3 cluster 4 (sparse slot) and M3 cluster 9 (dense slot), the two genomes of PR136's verdict. The hand-built generalist stays a positive control only (sensitivity check, neutral fixtures, tau); it does not vote. The candidate lists are singletons (`sparse [4]`, `dense [9]`): if a genome fails the viability gate under the new seeds there is no substitution (no c10, no c16). The existing rule then applies: no qualifying genome is `measurement-limited`, one is a single-genome `inconclusive`.
- Operator: `mutateInPlace` through `editFromSlots`, magnitudes 6 and 24, matched multi-locus shapes (2 x 12, 4 x 6).
- Neighbourhood families: single6, single24, multi2, multi4 (the equal-slot-count secondary family never entered the rule and is not emitted in this panel).
- Scenarios, `ASSAY` values (tile 16, radius 3, horizon 200, founder, scenario parameters, gate 1000 / 250, `viableFloor` 500) except `seeds`; tau formula; `RULE` in full (deep-equality tests against PR136's recorded `protocol.rule` and the code constant). No threshold is moved.
- No physics, schema, golden or `RULE_VERSION` change. Protected paths are checked with `git diff --exit-code e56077bd`.

## 2. Fixed N, no optional stopping

**N = 192 assayed genuine non-duplicate edits per M3 genome** (8 for the generalist, as in PR136). The set is drawn and committed before the run (`freeze.json`); the run assays exactly that set once. A crashed or failed run is rerun from scratch with the same frozen set. The set is never extended, reduced, re-drawn or cherry-picked after any outcome; a defect found before the run restarts from the freeze commit with a new dated amendment.

### 2.1 Why 192: a power argument from PR136's observed rate

PR136 observed A / N = 1/24 (4.2 percent) improving edits per genome. Illustrative model: A ~ Binomial(N, p) per genome, independent genomes. The model is illustrative, not a guarantee: the real draw is stratified without replacement, loci recur across the two magnitudes, and multi-locus edits share constituents (PR136 PLAN 2.5 noted these dependencies). Exact rational values (reproduced by `binomialAtLeast` in `packages/lineage/src/locality/panel.ts` and pinned in `packages/lineage/test/locality-power.test.ts`):

| true improving rate p | P(A >= 5), N = 192, one genome | both genomes | N = 144, one genome | both | N = 24 (PR136), one genome |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 6 percent | 0.990 | 0.982 | 0.937 | 0.878 | 0.012 |
| 1/24 (PR136) | **0.905** | **0.819** | 0.720 | 0.519 | 0.0027 |
| 3.5 percent | 0.804 | 0.647 | 0.569 | 0.324 | 0.0013 |
| 3 percent | 0.685 | 0.469 | 0.434 | 0.188 | 0.0006 |
| 2 percent | 0.339 | 0.115 | 0.163 | 0.026 | 0.0001 |

At the rate PR136 saw, the probability that A >= 5 on both genomes (so that the rule can leave its underpowered branch) is **0.82 at N = 192**, and under 0.01 at N = 24. N = 240 would give about 0.95 but is not affordable in the budget below. Power below p of about 3 percent is low: a second `inconclusive (underpowered)` is a legitimate outcome and is reported as such.

### 2.2 CPU budget and the deterministic choice of N

Budget: about 20 minutes on a loaded machine. Rule, applied to a baseline-only timing and fixed in code (`chooseN` in `tools/locality/power.ts`): N = 192 if the projected total is at most 1200 s, else N = 144 if that is, else stop and report without running (no third size). The timing workload is `power.ts --calibrate`: the start-genome baseline assays of the generalist, M3 c4 and M3 c9 under both seed pairs (36 runs) and one probe of each, median of 3 serial repetitions; it has no code path that assays an edited genome (a test asserts `mutantAssays` is 0 and the run count is 36). Projection = counted work times the median per-run and per-probe cost, where the counted work is 6 runs per assayed edit, the baselines, 100 runs for fixtures and slack, and every probe (each generated edit once, plus the null replicates).

Measured while writing this amendment on a machine at load average 20 to 30: about 0.13 s per run, 0.009 s per probe. Projection: **about 340 s at N = 192 and 270 s at N = 144 (freeze.json records its own re-measurement)**, both well inside 1200 s, so N = 192 is chosen. `freeze.json` re-measures and records the same numbers; the choice follows by the rule. Timing is provenance, not part of any deterministic payload.

**The worker pool of PLAN 3.3 is not built**: its own condition ("only if the serial projection exceeds the budget") is not met. `--workers n` is accepted, recorded in the manifest as requested 1 or 2 and used 1, and has no effect; serial is the reference path. The PLAN's 1-versus-2-workers equality test therefore does not exist.

### 2.3 What A >= 5 does and does not buy

`decide()` is unchanged. A >= 5 on both genomes moves the rule out of the `underpowered` guard into `broadly-useful`, `entanglement-signal`, or the residual `inconclusive` (the genomes disagree or neither rule is met). That residual `inconclusive` is a decided outcome of the rule, distinct from the underpowered one, and is reported as such.

- `broadly-useful` additionally needs K >= 10 percent of assayed genuine edits, i.e. K >= 20 at N = 192 (K >= 15 at 144), and K >= half of A, and a multi-locus clean share within 5 points of the single-locus one. Under the binomial model at p = 1/24, P(A >= 20) is 0.0002 at N = 192 (P(A >= 15) is 0.0011 at N = 144). A `broadly-useful` call is very unlikely unless the true improving rate is several times PR136's.
- `entanglement-signal` needs, on both genomes, collateral C >= half of A, median breadth (over all generated genuine single-locus w1/b1 edits, doubled units) >= 6 (3 actuators) and >= null + 2. An outcome-free preflight at freeze (`breadthUpperBound2` in `freeze.json`: the median of the wiring bound popcount of structurally reachable actuators over those edits, which bounds the realised breadth from above) gives **2 for M3 c4**: c4 cannot reach the breadth threshold on any outcome, so `entanglement-signal` is **unreachable on this pair**. For c9 the bound is 16 (reachable by the bound). This panel therefore cannot show entanglement and must not be advertised as able to; the achievable aim is to turn `inconclusive (underpowered)` into a mechanically decided `inconclusive (neither rule met)` or `broadly-useful`, and to report A, C, K with the finite-panel counts. Testing entanglement would need a different genome or a rule change, each a separately versioned protocol (not done here).

### 2.4 Pre-stated reading of the result for the go/no-go

- `broadly-useful`: no-go for an architecture fork; better search and incentives first (the page's own wording).
- `entanglement-signal`: advisory go for a separate, separately versioned study of a representation fork (unreachable on c4, so not expected).
- `inconclusive` (any branch), `measurement-limited`: no-go on this evidence; not a refutation. If still underpowered (A < 5 on a genome) RESULT.md says so and does not turn the low rate into a null.
- In every case exploratory and bounded to these two genomes, the one-step operator, the 26,244-point probe grid and the three 16x16 tiny-world biomass assays; any controller-layout change needs its own versioned plan, migration and CPU/WGSL parity.

## 3. The larger neighbourhood and the allocation rule

New panel `power-v1` widens only the draw counts of the same families: single-locus slots per kind `w1 40, b1 8, w2 32, b2 8` (the b kinds exhaust their pools) plus the 3 physics slots, both magnitudes, sign by counter draw; `drawsPerMulti` 64 for multi2 and for multi4.

**Allocation rule (a structural rule written before any pool was counted; not tuned on PR136 improvement counts).** Equal share N/4 for each family (single6, single24, multi2, multi4). Within a single-locus family the share is split over w1, b1, w2, b2, physics proportionally to the ELIGIBLE pool sizes by largest remainder (ties by that order). A kind is capped at its pool and the deficit moves to w1 of the same family, then w2, b1, b2, physics while capacity remains. A multi family takes min(share, pool); its deficit moves to the other multi family, then to w1 of single24, then of single6. If the total cannot be placed the freeze stops. The allocation is recomputed from the saved rows by `--check` and must equal the frozen one.

Pools (genuine non-duplicate edits per stratum; "eligible" excludes edits whose genome hash occurs among PR136's edits of the same start genome, see section 4) and the rule's output at both sizes:

| stratum | c4 generated | c4 eligible | c4 N=192 | c4 N=144 | c9 generated | c9 eligible | c9 N=192 | c9 N=144 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| single6/w1 | 40 | 36 | 22 | 16 | 40 | 37 | 23 | 17 |
| single6/b1 | 8 | 4 | 2 | 2 | 8 | 6 | 4 | 3 |
| single6/w2 | 32 | 31 | 19 | 14 | 32 | 28 | 17 | 13 |
| single6/b2 | 8 | 7 | 4 | 3 | 8 | 4 | 3 | 2 |
| single6/physics | 2 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 1 |
| single24/w1 | 40 | 36 | 22 | 17 | 40 | 36 | 23 | 17 |
| single24/b1 | 8 | 6 | 4 | 3 | 8 | 7 | 4 | 3 |
| single24/w2 | 32 | 31 | 19 | 14 | 32 | 26 | 17 | 13 |
| single24/b2 | 8 | 4 | 3 | 2 | 8 | 4 | 3 | 2 |
| single24/physics | 2 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 1 |
| multi2/multi | 63 | 63 | 48 | 36 | 64 | 64 | 48 | 36 |
| multi4/multi | 58 | 58 | 48 | 36 | 64 | 64 | 48 | 36 |

The generalist (control, 8 edits: single24 w1 2, w2 2, b1 1, b2 1, physics 1, multi2 1) has enough eligible edits in every stratum except single24/physics (eligible 1, needs 1), which is enough.

The frozen allocations (checked against `freeze.json` by a test):

```json
{
 "m3-c4": {
  "144": {
   "multi2/multi": 36,
   "multi4/multi": 36,
   "single24/b1": 3,
   "single24/b2": 2,
   "single24/w1": 17,
   "single24/w2": 14,
   "single6/b1": 2,
   "single6/b2": 3,
   "single6/physics": 1,
   "single6/w1": 16,
   "single6/w2": 14
  },
  "192": {
   "multi2/multi": 48,
   "multi4/multi": 48,
   "single24/b1": 4,
   "single24/b2": 3,
   "single24/w1": 22,
   "single24/w2": 19,

   "multi4/multi": 36,
   "single24/b1": 3,

```

**Estimand change, declared.** The multi-locus share of the assayed set is 96/192 (PR136: 6/24 per genome) and physics edits fall to 1 or 2 of 192 (PR136: 2/24); both PR136 improvements were multi2 edits, so a larger multi share could raise A. The allocation follows the structural rule above, not those counts, but the effect exists. The verdict uses unweighted A, C, K and the unweighted multi-versus-single clean shares, and the breadth median includes unassayed edits, so the same numeric thresholds apply to a different mixture. This is "the same rule read on a larger, differently mixed, fresh panel"; the result is a statement about this panel's eligible neighbourhood, not about PR136's. Inverse-probability-weighted estimates (eligible population over chosen, per stratum) are reported alongside.

## 4. New disjoint seeds, fresh edits, eligibility

- Assay seeds `[101, 102]` (PR136: `[1, 2]`); same count, so the `3 x seeds` term of tau and the viable floor keep their meaning.
- Neighbourhood and selection salt `0x1c0de06` (PR136: `0x1c0de05`); degree-matched null replicate tags prefixed `power:` (`power:<genome>:null:<r>`).
- Every edit whose genome hash occurs anywhere in PR136's 94-edit neighbourhood of the same start genome (assayed or not) is excluded from the draw: zero inclusion probability. The evidence records `generated` (full, includes the excluded rows; used for breadth and descriptors) and `eligible` per stratum; selection, weighting and the population recomputation in `--check` use the same predicate; the exclusion lists are stored verbatim in the panel record, so `--check` does not need PR136 to recompute them (it compares them to PR136's saved hashes when present). Estimates describe the eligible complement.
- Tests: seeds disjoint, salt different, null namespace different, no assayed hash shared with any PR136 row; independent negative fixtures (overlapping seeds only, equal salt only, equal namespace only, one overlapping hash only, each failing for its own reason).
- Baselines, gate and tau under the new seeds are computed from baselines only (the same code) at freeze and written to `freeze.json`. They are baseline facts, not mutant outcomes.

## 5. The new panel is read on its own

PR136's 24 edits are NOT pooled: different seeds hence different baselines, tau and gate, and a different neighbourhood. No cross-panel statistic enters the decision. The page and RESULT.md show the two panels side by side only.

## 6. Procedure and guards

1. Library parameterisation (`Panel`; `PANEL_V1` is today's constants and PR136 `--replay-check` stays byte-identical, gate passed before this amendment: 59 s under Deno). Because `tools/locality/matrix.ts --check` hashes the locality sources, adding `panel.ts` and editing the others changes those hashes: only `manifest.sources` of PR136 is re-pinned, after the replay proved `evidence.json` byte-identical. `evidence.json` and its seal are untouched.
2. `power.ts --freeze` writes `freeze.json` (sealed): N, allocations at both sizes, panel record with exclusion lists, baselines, gate, tau, chosen edit ids and hashes, fixture inventory, preflight, timing, SHA-256 of this file, of PR136's `evidence.json` and of the source closure (library, `genotype.ts`, `tools/locality/matrix.ts`, `tools/locality/power.ts`).
3. A Codex Sol 6.1 xhigh review of this amendment and the freeze; defects fixed; then ONLY the freeze files (this amendment, PLAN.md, REVIEW.md, `freeze.json`, the code and tests, PR136's re-pinned manifest) are committed and pushed to `claude/i05-power`. No outcome file is in that commit.
4. `power.ts --write` refuses if `freeze.json` is missing, its seal fails, any frozen input or source differs from its frozen hash, any of them differs from `HEAD`, the freeze commit is not an ancestor of `origin/claude/i05-power`, or `evidence.json` exists. Before the first edited genome is assayed it rebuilds the neighbourhoods and asserts that the drawn ids and hashes equal the frozen set. It writes `evidence.json` and `manifest.json` once.
5. `--check` (no simulation: seal, freeze match, exact fixture inventory, recomputation of every derived field, the allocation from the eligible pools, disjointness, the verdict) and `--replay-check` (regenerate and require byte equality). The verdict is `decide()` on the saved rows, applied mechanically; RESULT.md reports A, C, K, the verdict, the go/no-go of section 2.4, deviations.
6. Page: `/controller-locality/` gets a panel selector and a side-by-side table. No physics, schema or golden change; no paid calls; no deploy.

## 7. Limits

Biomass responses are not independent capabilities (as in PR136). Short horizon (200), tiny tile, two seeds, one-step operator, two genomes. Not a claim about evolved creatures in general. Even with A >= 5 the go/no-go is advisory.

  * The neighbourhood of `base`: single-locus edits (per-kind stratified slot sample, two signed magnitudes), the matched-magnitude

    37	/** PR136's genome hashes per start genome (every edit it generated, assayed or not) and the assayed ones. */
    38	export function earlierHashes(pr136Path = PR136_EVIDENCE): { all: Record<string, string[]>; assayed: Record<string, string[]>; seeds: number[]; salt: number } {
    39	  const p = JSON.parse(readFileSync(pr136Path, "utf8")) as Payload;
    40	  const all: Record<string, string[]> = {}, assayed: Record<string, string[]> = {};
    41	  for (const r of p.rows) {
    42	    (all[r.genome] ??= []).push(r.hash);
    43	    if (r.assay) (assayed[r.genome] ??= []).push(r.hash);
    44	  }
    45	  for (const m of [all, assayed]) for (const k of Object.keys(m)) m[k] = [...new Set(m[k])].sort();
    46	  const proto = p.protocol as { assay: { seeds: number[] }; neighbourhood: { salt: number } };
    47	  return { all, assayed, seeds: proto.assay.seeds, salt: proto.neighbourhood.salt };
    48	}

    50	// ---- Calibration (baseline only) -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    51	
    52	const median = (a: number[]): number => [...a].sort((x, y) => x - y)[Math.floor(a.length / 2)];
    53	
    54	export interface Calibration {
    55	  note: string;
    56	  repetitions: number;
    57	  baselineRuns: number;
    58	  /** Mutant assays performed by the calibration. Always 0: it has no code path that assays an edited genome. */
    59	  mutantAssays: number;
    60	  secondsPerRunMedian: number;
    61	  secondsPerProbeMedian: number;
    62	}
    63	
    64	/** Baseline-only workload: the start-genome baseline assays of the generalist, M3 c4 and M3 c9 under both seed pairs, and one probe of each. */
    65	export function calibrate(repetitions = 3): Calibration {
    66	  const starts = [lib.generalistStart(), lib.m3Start(4, "m3-sparse"), lib.m3Start(9, "m3-dense")];
    67	  const pairs: readonly (readonly number[])[] = [lib.ASSAY.seeds, lib.POWER_SEEDS];
    68	  const runT: number[] = [], probeT: number[] = [];
    69	  let runs = 0;
    70	  for (let r = 0; r < repetitions; r++) {
    71	    const cache = new lib.AssayCache();
    72	    const t0 = performance.now();
    73	    for (const s of starts) for (const seeds of pairs) lib.baselineOf(cache, s.words, seeds);
    74	    runT.push((performance.now() - t0) / 1000 / cache.runs);
    75	    runs = cache.runs;
    76	    const t1 = performance.now();
    77	    for (const s of starts) lib.probeGenome(s.words);
    78	    probeT.push((performance.now() - t1) / 1000 / starts.length);
    79	  }
    80	  return { note: "timing is provenance, not part of any deterministic payload", repetitions, baselineRuns: runs, mutantAssays: 0, secondsPerRunMedian: median(runT), secondsPerProbeMedian: median(probeT) };
    81	}
    82	
    83	export interface Workload { assayRuns: number; probes: number }
    84	/** Projected seconds of the whole run: simulation runs plus controller probes, from counted work and the calibration. */
    85	export const projectedSeconds = (w: Workload, c: Calibration): number => w.assayRuns * c.secondsPerRunMedian + w.probes * c.secondsPerProbeMedian;
    86	/** The deterministic N rule: the first candidate within budget, else null (stop and report without running; no third N). */
    87	export function chooseN(projected: Record<number, number>): number | null {
    88	  for (const n of CANDIDATE_N) if (projected[n] <= BUDGET_SECONDS) return n;
    89	  return null;
    90	}

    98	  generated: Record<string, number>;
    99	  eligible: Record<string, number>;
   100	  allocation: Allocation;
   101	  chosen: [string, string][];
   102	  /** Outcome-free upper bound of the median breadth (doubled) of genuine single-locus w1/b1 edits: the wiring bound popcount(structActuators). */
   103	  breadthUpperBound2: number;
   104	  entanglementReachable: boolean;
   105	}

   121	  calibration: Calibration;
   122	  baselineAssayRuns: number;
   123	  inputs: { pr136EvidenceSha256: string; amendmentSha256: string };
   124	  sources: Record<string, string>;
   125	  integritySha256?: string;
   126	}
   127	
   128	const median2 = (a: number[]): number => lib.median2(a);
   129	
   130	/** Everything the run needs, computed without assaying any edited genome. `n` forces a size (tests, --dry); otherwise the rule picks it. */
   131	export function planFreeze(calibration: Calibration, forceN?: number, say: (m: string) => void = () => {}): Freeze {
   132	  const earlier = earlierHashes();
   133	  const cache = new lib.AssayCache();
   134	  const qualPanel = lib.powerPanel(earlier.all, {});
   135	  const q = lib.qualifyStarts(qualPanel, cache, say);
   136	  const baselineAssayRuns = cache.runs;
   137	  const starts = [q.gen, ...q.chosenStarts];
   138	  const nbs = new Map<string, import("@bl/lineage").Neighbourhood>();
   139	  const pools = new Map<string, { generated: Record<string, number>; eligible: Record<string, number> }>();
   140	  const workload: Record<string, Workload> = {};
   141	  for (const s of starts) {
   142	    const nb = lib.buildNeighbourhood(s.words, s.id, undefined, lib.POWER_NB);
   143	    nbs.set(s.id, nb);
   144	    const ex = lib.excludeSet(qualPanel, s.id);
   145	    const { generated, byStratum } = lib.strataPools(nb, ex);
   146	    pools.set(s.id, { generated, eligible: Object.fromEntries([...byStratum].map(([k, v]) => [k, v.length])) });
   147	  }
   148	  const probeCount = (): number => starts.reduce((a, s) => a + nbs.get(s.id)!.edits.length + lib.RULE.nullReplicates * nbs.get(s.id)!.edits.filter((e) => e.k === 1 && (e.kind === "w1" || e.kind === "b1")).length, 0);
   149	  const allocations: Freeze["allocations"] = {};
   150	  const projected: Record<string, number> = {};
   151	  const projectedByN: Record<number, number> = {};
   152	  for (const n of CANDIDATE_N) {
   153	    let mutants = 0;
   154	    for (const s of starts) {
   155	      if (s.role === "control") { mutants += Object.values(lib.POWER_ALLOC_CONTROL).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0); continue; }
   156	      const al = lib.deriveAllocation(n, pools.get(s.id)!.eligible);
   157	      (allocations[s.id] ??= {})[String(n)] = al;
   158	      mutants += n;
   159	    }
   160	    workload[String(n)] = { assayRuns: mutants * lib.SCENARIO_IDS.length * lib.ASSAY.seeds.length + baselineAssayRuns + FIXTURE_AND_SLACK_RUNS, probes: probeCount() };
   161	    projected[String(n)] = Math.round(projectedSeconds(workload[String(n)], calibration));
   162	    projectedByN[n] = projected[String(n)];
   163	  }
   164	  const n = forceN ?? chooseN(projectedByN);
   165	  if (n === null) throw new Error(`projected ${JSON.stringify(projected)} s exceeds the ${BUDGET_SECONDS} s budget at every size: stop and report without running`);
   166	  const allocByGenome: Record<string, Allocation> = {};
   167	  for (const s of q.chosenStarts) allocByGenome[s.id] = allocations[s.id][String(n)];
   168	  const panel = lib.powerPanel(earlier.all, allocByGenome);
   169	  const plans: GenomePlan[] = starts.map((s) => {
   170	    const nb = nbs.get(s.id)!;
   171	    const alloc = lib.allocFor(panel, s.id, s.role === "control");
   172	    const { chosen } = lib.drawAssaySubset(nb, alloc, panel.nb.salt, lib.excludeSet(panel, s.id));
   173	    const wb = nb.edits.filter((e) => e.cls === "genuine" && e.k === 1 && (e.kind === "w1" || e.kind === "b1"));
   174	    const bound = wb.map((e) => {
   175	      const i = nb.edits.indexOf(e);
   176	      return lib.popcount(lib.structuralReach(s.words, e.slots.map((x) => x.slot)).actuators | lib.structuralReach(nb.words[i], e.slots.map((x) => x.slot)).actuators);
   177	    });
   178	    const bound2 = median2(bound);
   179	    const rec = q.genomes.find((g) => g.id === s.id)!;
   180	    return {
   181	      id: s.id, votes: rec.votes, generated: pools.get(s.id)!.generated, eligible: pools.get(s.id)!.eligible, allocation: alloc,
   182	      chosen: chosen.map((i): [string, string] => [nb.edits[i].id, nb.edits[i].hash]),
   183	      breadthUpperBound2: bound2,
   184	      entanglementReachable: rec.votes ? bound2 >= 2 * lib.RULE.entanglement.minMedianBreadthActuators : false,
   185	    };
   186	  });
   187	  for (const p of plans) {
   188	    const have = Object.values(p.allocation).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), got = p.chosen.length;
   189	    if (have !== got) throw new Error(`${p.id}: drew ${got} edits for an allocation of ${have}`);
   190	  }
   191	  const fixtureInventory = ["generalist-hidden-permutation", "generalist-dead-hidden-edit", "generalist-clamp-edit", "generalist-shuffled-sensor-labels", "generalist-localised-edit", ...q.chosenStarts.filter((s) => lib.widestPermutation(s.words) !== null).map((s) => `${s.id}-hidden-permutation`)];
   192	  const freeze: Freeze = {
   193	    kind: "controller-locality-power-freeze", version: 1,
   194	    statement: "Written before any edited genome of this panel was assayed. Baselines only: no mutant outcome exists when this file is made.",
   195	    n, allocations, panel, genomes: q.genomes, candidates: q.candidates, plans, fixtureInventory, workload, projectedSeconds: projected, calibration, baselineAssayRuns,
   196	    inputs: { pr136EvidenceSha256: sha256(readFileSync(PR136_EVIDENCE)), amendmentSha256: existsSync(AMENDMENT) ? sha256(readFileSync(AMENDMENT)) : "absent" },
   197	    sources: sourceHashes(EXTRA_SOURCES),
   198	  };
   199	  return freeze;
   200	}
   201	
   202	export const sealFreeze = (f: Freeze): Freeze => { const { integritySha256: _d, ...rest } = f; return { ...rest, integritySha256: sha256(canonical(rest)) }; };
   203	export const verifyFreezeSeal = (f: Freeze): boolean => { const { integritySha256, ...rest } = f; return integritySha256 === sha256(canonical(rest)); };
   204	export const readFreeze = (path = FREEZE): Freeze => JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8")) as Freeze;
   205	
   206	/** Recompute the freeze from scratch (no mutant assay) and compare to the saved one, ignoring only the timing provenance. */
   207	export function freezeCheck(path = FREEZE): string[] {
   208	  if (!existsSync(path)) return ["freeze.json is missing"];
   209	  const saved = readFreeze(path);
   210	  const out: string[] = [];
     1	// Panels of the I05 locality study (I05b power-up, docs/controller-locality-power-2026-11-02/AMENDMENT.md).
     2	// A panel fixes everything that differs between the original assay and the larger one: salt, assay seeds, draw counts,
     3	// allocation, candidate list, null tag namespace and the PR136 hashes excluded from the draw. The decision rule, the
     4	// operator, the scenarios, tau and the gate are NOT panel parameters. Pure, integer and browser-safe.
     5	import type { Allocation, NbParams } from "./neighbourhood.ts";
     6	
     7	export type SlotSpec = { slot: "sparse" | "dense"; clusters: number[] };
     8	
     9	export interface Panel {
    10	  id: string;
    11	  nb: NbParams;
    12	  /** Assay seeds. The count is part of tau and the viable floor, so a panel must keep the original count. */
    13	  seeds: readonly number[];
    14	  allocM3: Allocation;
    15	  allocControl: Allocation;
    16	  /** Per start genome allocation, when pools differ between genomes (overrides allocM3 / allocControl). */
    17	  allocByGenome?: Record<string, Allocation>;
    18	  slots: SlotSpec[];
    19	  /** Prefix of the degree-matched null replicate tags (`${prefix}${genome}:null:${r}`). */
    20	  nullPrefix: string;
    21	  /** Genome id -> sorted genome hashes (any earlier panel's edits of that start genome) that have zero inclusion probability. */
    22	  exclude: Record<string, string[]>;
    23	}
    24	
    25	export const allocFor = (panel: Panel, genomeId: string, control: boolean): Allocation => panel.allocByGenome?.[genomeId] ?? (control ? panel.allocControl : panel.allocM3);
    26	export const excludeSet = (panel: Panel, genomeId: string): ReadonlySet<string> | undefined => (panel.exclude[genomeId]?.length ? new Set(panel.exclude[genomeId]) : undefined);
    27	
    28	// ---- The power panel --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29	
    30	export const POWER_ID = "power-v1";
    31	export const POWER_SALT = 0x1c0de06;
    32	export const POWER_SEEDS = [101, 102] as const;
    33	/** Same families as PR136, wider draws: the b kinds exhaust their pools (8 hidden units, 8 actuators). */
    34	export const POWER_NB: NbParams = { salt: POWER_SALT, singleSlots: { w1: 40, b1: 8, w2: 32, b2: 8 }, drawsPerMulti: 64, secondary: false };
    35	export const POWER_NULL_PREFIX = "power:";
    36	/** One candidate per slot: if a genome fails the gate under the new seeds there is no substitution. */
    37	export const POWER_SLOTS: SlotSpec[] = [{ slot: "sparse", clusters: [4] }, { slot: "dense", clusters: [9] }];
    38	/** Control (generalist) allocation is PR136's, 8 edits: it serves the sensitivity and neutral controls only. */
    39	export const POWER_ALLOC_CONTROL: Allocation = { "single24/w1": 2, "single24/w2": 2, "single24/b1": 1, "single24/b2": 1, "single24/physics": 1, "multi2/multi": 1 };
    40	
    41	export const SINGLE_FAMILIES = ["single6", "single24"] as const;
    42	export const MULTI_FAMILIES = ["multi2", "multi4"] as const;
    43	/** Fixed order of the single-locus kinds: used for ties in rounding and as the spill order of a deficit. */
    44	export const KIND_ORDER = ["w1", "b1", "w2", "b2", "physics"] as const;
    45	
    46	/**
    47	 * The structural allocation rule, written before any pool was counted and independent of any outcome.
    48	 * Per family an equal share N/4 (families single6, single24, multi2, multi4). Within a single-locus family, the share is split
    49	 * over the five kinds proportionally to their ELIGIBLE pool sizes by largest remainder (ties by KIND_ORDER); a kind is capped at
    50	 * its pool, and the deficit is moved to w1 of the same family, then to w2, b1, b2, physics while capacity remains.
    51	 * A multi family takes min(share, pool); its deficit is moved to the other multi family, then to w1 of single24, then of single6.
    52	 * Throws when the total cannot be placed (the freeze then stops; no third path).
    53	 */
    54	export function deriveAllocation(n: number, pools: Record<string, number>): Allocation {
    55	  if (n % 4 !== 0) throw new Error("N must be divisible by 4 (equal share per family)");
    56	  const share = n / 4;
    57	  const alloc: Allocation = {};
    58	  const cap = (k: string) => pools[k] ?? 0;
    59	  for (const fam of SINGLE_FAMILIES) {
    60	    const keys = KIND_ORDER.map((k) => `${fam}/${k}`);
    61	    const total = keys.reduce((a, k) => a + cap(k), 0);
    62	    if (total === 0) throw new Error(`no eligible edits in ${fam}`);
    63	    // Largest-remainder quotas (integer arithmetic).
    64	    const quota = keys.map((k) => Math.floor((share * cap(k)) / total));
    65	    let left = share - quota.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    66	    const order = keys.map((k, i) => ({ i, rem: (share * cap(k)) % total })).sort((a, b) => b.rem - a.rem || a.i - b.i);
    67	    for (const { i } of order) { if (left === 0) break; quota[i]++; left--; }
    68	    let deficit = 0;
    69	    keys.forEach((k, i) => { const t = Math.min(quota[i], cap(k)); deficit += quota[i] - t; quota[i] = t; });
    70	    for (const i of [0, 2, 1, 3, 4]) { // w1, w2, b1, b2, physics
    71	      const room = cap(keys[i]) - quota[i], mv = Math.min(room, deficit);
    72	      quota[i] += mv; deficit -= mv;
    73	    }
    74	    if (deficit) throw new Error(`${fam}: cannot place ${deficit} edits within its pools`);
    75	    keys.forEach((k, i) => { if (quota[i] > 0) alloc[k] = quota[i]; });
    76	  }
    77	  let spill = 0;
    78	  const multi: Record<string, number> = {};
    79	  for (const fam of MULTI_FAMILIES) { const k = `${fam}/multi`; multi[k] = Math.min(share, cap(k)); spill += share - multi[k]; }
    80	  for (const k of Object.keys(multi)) { const mv = Math.min(spill, cap(k) - multi[k]); multi[k] += mv; spill -= mv; }
    81	  for (const fam of ["single24", "single6"]) { const k = `${fam}/w1`, mv = Math.min(spill, cap(k) - (alloc[k] ?? 0)); if (mv) alloc[k] = (alloc[k] ?? 0) + mv; spill -= mv; }
    82	  if (spill) throw new Error(`cannot place ${spill} edits within the pools`);
    83	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(multi)) if (v > 0) alloc[k] = v;
    84	  const sum = Object.values(alloc).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    85	  if (sum !== n) throw new Error(`allocation sums to ${sum}, not ${n}`);
    86	  return alloc;
    87	}
    88	
    89	export function powerPanel(exclude: Record<string, string[]>, allocByGenome: Record<string, Allocation>): Panel {
    90	  return { id: POWER_ID, nb: POWER_NB, seeds: POWER_SEEDS, allocM3: {}, allocControl: POWER_ALLOC_CONTROL, allocByGenome, slots: POWER_SLOTS, nullPrefix: POWER_NULL_PREFIX, exclude };
    91	}

    93	// ---- Disjointness from an earlier panel ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    94	
    95	/** Each reason is independent, so a fixture that overlaps in one respect only fails for that reason. */
    96	export function disjointnessProblems(panel: Panel, earlier: { seeds: readonly number[]; salt: number; nullPrefix: string; hashes: Record<string, readonly string[]> }, assayedHashes: Record<string, readonly string[]>): string[] {
    97	  const out: string[] = [];
    98	  const seeds = new Set(earlier.seeds);
    99	  if (panel.seeds.some((s) => seeds.has(s))) out.push("assay seeds overlap the earlier panel's");
   100	  if (panel.nb.salt === earlier.salt) out.push("neighbourhood salt equals the earlier panel's");
   101	  if (panel.nullPrefix === earlier.nullPrefix) out.push("null replicate namespace equals the earlier panel's");
   102	  if (panel.seeds.length !== earlier.seeds.length) out.push("seed count differs from the earlier panel's (tau and the viable floor use it)");
   103	  for (const [g, hs] of Object.entries(assayedHashes)) {
   104	    const prior = new Set(earlier.hashes[g] ?? []);
   105	    if (hs.some((h) => prior.has(h))) out.push(`${g}: an assayed genome hash occurs among the earlier panel's edits`);
   106	  }
   107	  return out;
   108	}
   109	
   110	// ---- Exact binomial tail --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   111	
   112	const C = (n: number, k: number): bigint => { let r = 1n; for (let i = 1; i <= k; i++) r = (r * BigInt(n - k + i)) / BigInt(i); return r; };
   113	/** P(X >= k) for X ~ Binomial(n, pn/pd), as an exact rational {num, den}. */
   114	export function binomialAtLeast(n: number, k: number, pn: number, pd: number): { num: bigint; den: bigint } {
   115	  const den = BigInt(pd) ** BigInt(n);
   116	  let below = 0n;
   117	  for (let a = 0; a < k; a++) below += C(n, a) * BigInt(pn) ** BigInt(a) * BigInt(pd - pn) ** BigInt(n - a);
   118	  return { num: den - below, den };
   119	}
   120	/** The probability rounded down to `digits` decimals, as a string such as "0.905". */
   121	export function decimal(r: { num: bigint; den: bigint }, digits: number): string {
   122	  const scaled = (r.num * 10n ** BigInt(digits)) / r.den;
   123	  const s = scaled.toString().padStart(digits + 1, "0");
   124	  return `${s.slice(0, s.length - digits)}.${s.slice(s.length - digits)}`;
   125	}

   206	/** Recompute the freeze from scratch (no mutant assay) and compare to the saved one, ignoring only the timing provenance. */
   207	export function freezeCheck(path = FREEZE): string[] {
   208	  if (!existsSync(path)) return ["freeze.json is missing"];
   209	  const saved = readFreeze(path);
   210	  const out: string[] = [];
   211	  if (!verifyFreezeSeal(saved)) out.push("freeze integritySha256 does not match");
   212	  const again = planFreeze(saved.calibration, saved.n);
   213	  const strip = (f: Freeze) => { const { integritySha256: _s, ...r } = f; return r; };
   214	  if (canonical(strip(sealFreeze(again))) !== canonical(strip(saved))) out.push("recomputed freeze differs from freeze.json");
   215	  if (!CANDIDATE_N.includes(saved.n as 192 | 144)) out.push(`N ${saved.n} is not one of the two pre-stated sizes`);
   216	  if (chooseN(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(saved.projectedSeconds).map(([k, v]) => [Number(k), v]))) !== saved.n) out.push("N does not follow from the projected times by the stated rule");
   217	  return out;
   218	}

   222	const git = (args: string[]): string => execFileSync("git", args, { cwd: REPO, encoding: "utf8", stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"] }).trim();
   223	export function committedProblems(paths: readonly string[], requirePushed: boolean): string[] {
   224	  const out: string[] = [];
   225	  for (const p of paths) {
   226	    try { git(["ls-files", "--error-unmatch", p]); } catch { out.push(`${p} is not tracked by git`); continue; }
   227	    try { git(["diff", "--quiet", "HEAD", "--", p]); } catch { out.push(`${p} differs from HEAD`); }
   228	  }
   229	  if (requirePushed) {
   230	    try {
   231	      const last = git(["log", "-1", "--format=%H", "--", "docs/controller-locality-power-2026-11-02/freeze.json"]);
   232	      if (!last) out.push("freeze.json has no commit");
   233	      else git(["merge-base", "--is-ancestor", last, `origin/${BRANCH}`]);
   234	    } catch { out.push(`the freeze commit is not an ancestor of origin/${BRANCH}: push it before running`); }
   235	  }
   236	  return out;
   237	}

   239	export const freezeInputPaths = (): string[] => [...sourceFiles(REPO, EXTRA_SOURCES), "docs/controller-locality-power-2026-11-02/AMENDMENT.md", "docs/controller-locality-power-2026-11-02/freeze.json", "docs/controller-locality-2026-11-01/evidence.json"];
   240	
   241	/** Why `--write` must refuse right now (empty when it may proceed). */
   242	export function writeRefusals(opts: { requirePushed?: boolean; freezePath?: string; evidencePath?: string } = {}): string[] {
   243	  const freezePath = opts.freezePath ?? FREEZE, evidencePath = opts.evidencePath ?? POWER_EVIDENCE;
   244	  if (!existsSync(freezePath)) return ["freeze.json is missing: run --freeze, review, commit and push it first"];
   245	  const f = readFreeze(freezePath);
   246	  const out: string[] = [];
   247	  if (!verifyFreezeSeal(f)) out.push("freeze.json seal fails");
   248	  const now = sourceHashes(EXTRA_SOURCES);
   249	  for (const k of new Set([...Object.keys(now), ...Object.keys(f.sources)])) if (now[k] !== f.sources[k]) out.push(`source ${k} differs from the frozen hash`);
   250	  if (f.inputs.pr136EvidenceSha256 !== sha256(readFileSync(PR136_EVIDENCE))) out.push("PR136 evidence differs from the frozen hash");
   251	  if (!existsSync(AMENDMENT) || f.inputs.amendmentSha256 !== sha256(readFileSync(AMENDMENT))) out.push("AMENDMENT.md differs from the frozen hash");
   252	  if (existsSync(evidencePath)) out.push("evidence.json exists: files are written once");
   253	  out.push(...committedProblems(freezeInputPaths(), opts.requirePushed ?? true));
   254	  return out;
   255	}

   259	export function generate(f: Freeze, onProgress?: (m: string) => void): Payload {
   260	  const frozen: import("@bl/lineage").FrozenChoice = Object.fromEntries(f.plans.map((p) => [p.id, p.chosen]));
   261	  const study = lib.runStudy({ panel: f.panel, frozen, onProgress });
   262	  const payload = { ...lib.buildPayload(study), freezeSha256: sha256(readFileSync(FREEZE)) } as Payload;
   263	  const { integritySha256: _d, ...rest } = payload;
   264	  return { ...rest, integritySha256: sha256(canonical(rest)) } as Payload;
   265	}
   266	
   267	export const checkOptions = (f: Freeze): CheckOptions => ({ extraSources: EXTRA_SOURCES, fixtureInventory: f.fixtureInventory });
   268	
   269	/** Freeze-bound checks on a saved payload (no simulation), in addition to the shared integrity and recomputation checks. */
   270	export function powerProblems(payload: Payload, f: Freeze, pr136Path = PR136_EVIDENCE, freezePath = FREEZE): string[] {
   271	  const out: string[] = [];
   272	  if (!payload.panel) return [...out, "payload carries no panel record"];
   273	  if (canonical(payload.panel) !== canonical(f.panel)) out.push("payload panel differs from the frozen panel");
   274	  if (canonical(payload.genomes) !== canonical(f.genomes)) out.push("genome baselines, tau or qualification differ from the frozen ones");
   275	  if (canonical(payload.candidates) !== canonical(f.candidates)) out.push("candidates differ from the frozen ones");
   276	  for (const p of f.plans) {
   277	    const got = payload.rows.filter((r) => r.genome === p.id && r.assay).map((r): [string, string] => [r.id, r.hash]);
   278	    if (canonical(got) !== canonical(p.chosen)) out.push(`${p.id}: the assayed set differs from the frozen one`);
   279	    const want = Object.values(p.allocation).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
   280	    if (got.length !== want) out.push(`${p.id}: ${got.length} assayed edits, frozen ${want}`);
   281	    if (p.votes && got.length !== f.n) out.push(`${p.id}: ${got.length} assayed edits, fixed N is ${f.n}`);
   282	    const strata = payload.strata[p.id] ?? {};
   283	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(p.allocation)) if (strata[k]?.chosen !== v) out.push(`${p.id}: stratum ${k} chosen ${strata[k]?.chosen}, frozen ${v}`);
   284	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(p.eligible)) if (payload.strata[p.id]?.[k] && payload.strata[p.id][k].population !== v) out.push(`${p.id}: stratum ${k} eligible population differs from the frozen one`);
   285	    // The allocation is the stated rule's output on the recomputed eligible pools.
   286	    if (p.votes) {
   287	      const el: Record<string, number> = {};
   288	      for (const r of payload.rows) if (r.genome === p.id && r.cls === "genuine" && r.dupOf === null && !payload.panel.exclude[p.id]?.includes(r.hash)) { const k = lib.strataKey(r as unknown as Parameters<typeof lib.strataKey>[0]); el[k] = (el[k] ?? 0) + 1; }
   289	      if (canonical(lib.deriveAllocation(f.n, el)) !== canonical(p.allocation)) out.push(`${p.id}: allocation does not follow from the eligible pools by the stated rule`);
   290	    }
   291	  }
   292	  // Disjointness from PR136, read from its saved evidence.
   293	  const prior = earlierHashes(pr136Path);
   294	  const assayed: Record<string, string[]> = {};
   295	  for (const r of payload.rows) if (r.assay) (assayed[r.genome] ??= []).push(r.hash);
   296	  out.push(...lib.disjointnessProblems(payload.panel, { seeds: prior.seeds, salt: prior.salt, nullPrefix: "", hashes: prior.all }, assayed));
   297	  if (canonical(payload.panel.exclude) !== canonical(prior.all)) out.push("panel exclusion lists differ from PR136's recorded hashes");
   298	  if (payload.freezeSha256 !== sha256(readFileSync(freezePath))) out.push("payload freezeSha256 differs from freeze.json");
   299	  return out;
   300	}
   301	
   302	export function checkPower(evidence = POWER_EVIDENCE, manifest = POWER_MANIFEST, root = REPO, freezePath = FREEZE): string[] {
   303	  if (!existsSync(freezePath)) return ["freeze.json is missing"];
   304	  const f = readFreeze(freezePath);
   305	  const out: string[] = [];
   306	  if (!verifyFreezeSeal(f)) out.push("freeze integritySha256 does not match");
   307	  out.push(...checkSaved(evidence, manifest, root, checkOptions(f)));
   308	  if (existsSync(evidence)) {
   309	    const p = JSON.parse(readFileSync(evidence, "utf8")) as Payload;
   310	    if (!verifySeal(p)) return out;
   311	    out.push(...powerProblems(p, f, PR136_EVIDENCE, freezePath));
   312	  }
   313	  return out;
   314	}
   315	
   316	export function replayCheck(): string[] {
   317	  const f = readFreeze();
   318	  return serialize(generate(f)) === readFileSync(POWER_EVIDENCE, "utf8") ? [] : ["regenerated payload differs from evidence.json"];
   319	}
   320	
   321	// ---- CLI --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   322	
   323	function main(): void {
   324	  const mode = process.argv[2];
   325	  const arg = (name: string): string | undefined => { const i = process.argv.indexOf(name); return i >= 0 ? process.argv[i + 1] : undefined; };
   326	  const workers = Number(arg("--workers") ?? 1);
   327	  if (mode === "--calibrate") {
   328	    const c = calibrate();
   329	    console.log(JSON.stringify(c, null, 1));
   330	  } else if (mode === "--freeze") {
   331	    const dry = process.argv.includes("--dry");
   332	    if (!dry && existsSync(FREEZE)) throw new Error("freeze.json exists; it is written once. Amend by a new dated amendment, not by rewriting.");
   333	    const forced = arg("--n");
   334	    if (forced && !dry) throw new Error("--n is for --dry only");
   335	    const f = sealFreeze(planFreeze(calibrate(), forced ? Number(forced) : undefined, (m) => console.error(m)));
   336	    if (dry) console.log(JSON.stringify({ n: f.n, allocations: f.allocations, workload: f.workload, projectedSeconds: f.projectedSeconds, calibration: f.calibration, plans: f.plans.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, votes: p.votes, generated: p.generated, eligible: p.eligible, allocation: p.allocation, chosenCount: p.chosen.length, breadthUpperBound2: p.breadthUpperBound2, entanglementReachable: p.entanglementReachable })), genomes: f.genomes.map((g) => ({ id: g.id, baseline: g.baseline, tau: g.tau, qualification: g.qualification })), fixtureInventory: f.fixtureInventory }, null, 1));
   337	    else { writeFileSync(`${FREEZE}.tmp`, serialize(f)); renameSync(`${FREEZE}.tmp`, FREEZE); console.log(`wrote freeze.json N=${f.n} seal ${f.integritySha256}`); }
   338	  } else if (mode === "--freeze-check") {
   339	    const problems = freezeCheck();
   340	    if (problems.length) { console.error(problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   341	    console.log("--freeze-check ok");
   342	  } else if (mode === "--write") {
   343	    const problems = writeRefusals({ requirePushed: !process.argv.includes("--no-remote-check") });
   344	    if (problems.length) { console.error(`refusing to run:\n${problems.join("\n")}`); process.exit(1); }
   345	    const f = readFreeze();
   346	    const t = performance.now();
   347	    const payload = generate(f, (m) => console.error(m));
   348	    const text = serialize(payload);
   349	    const seconds = Math.round((performance.now() - t) / 1000);
   350	    const manifestText = serialize(makeManifest(payload, text, seconds, EXTRA_SOURCES, { freezeSha256: sha256(readFileSync(FREEZE)), n: f.n, workers: { requested: workers, used: 1, note: "worker pool not built: the serial projection is within the budget" } }));
   351	    writeFileSync(`${POWER_EVIDENCE}.tmp`, text);
   352	    writeFileSync(`${POWER_MANIFEST}.tmp`, manifestText);
   353	    renameSync(`${POWER_EVIDENCE}.tmp`, POWER_EVIDENCE);
   354	    renameSync(`${POWER_MANIFEST}.tmp`, POWER_MANIFEST);
   355	    console.log(`wrote evidence.json (${text.length} bytes) seal ${payload.integritySha256} in ${seconds} s`);
   356	  } else if (mode === "--check" || mode === "--replay-check") {
   357	    const problems = mode === "--check" ? checkPower() : replayCheck();
   358	    if (problems.length) { console.error(problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   359	    console.log(`${mode} ok`);
   360	  } else {
   361	    console.error("usage: deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/power.ts --calibrate | --freeze [--dry] | --freeze-check | --write | --check | --replay-check");
   362	    process.exit(2);
   363	  }
   364	}
   365	const isMain = (import.meta as { main?: boolean }).main ?? false;
   366	if (isMain) main();
     1	/**
     2	 * I05 controller-locality evidence. CPU only, no GPU. Exploratory; see docs/controller-locality-2026-11-01/PLAN.md.
     3	 *   deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/matrix.ts --write         write evidence.json and manifest.json (once, deliberately; --force rewrites)
     4	 *   deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/matrix.ts --check         validate the saved artifacts without simulating
     5	 *   deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/matrix.ts --replay-check  regenerate the payload and require byte equality
     6	 *   deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/matrix.ts --calibrate     print timings and the viability / noise numbers; writes nothing
     7	 * Also runs under Node 24 through the @bl/* resolver registered below.
     8	 */
     9	import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
    10	import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, renameSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
    11	import { registerHooks } from "node:module";
    12	import { arch, platform } from "node:os";
    13	import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from "node:url";
    14	
    15	export const REPO = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../", import.meta.url));
    16	export const OUT_DIR = `${REPO}docs/controller-locality-2026-11-01/`;
    17	export const EVIDENCE = `${OUT_DIR}evidence.json`;
    18	export const MANIFEST = `${OUT_DIR}manifest.json`;
    19	export const BASE_COMMIT = "b43db3be";
    20	const SRC_DIR = "packages/lineage/src/locality/";
    21	
    22	if (!(globalThis as { Deno?: unknown }).Deno && !process.env.VITEST) {
    23	  try {
    24	    registerHooks({
    25	      resolve(specifier, context, next) {
    26	        const m = /^@bl\/(.+)$/.exec(specifier);
    27	        return m ? next(pathToFileURL(`${REPO}packages/${m[1]}/src/index.ts`).href, context) : next(specifier, context);
    28	      },
    29	    });
    30	  } catch { /* a loader that resolves @bl/* is already active */ }
    31	}
    32	const lib = await import("@bl/lineage");
    33	type Payload = import("@bl/lineage").Payload;
    34	
    35	export const sha256 = (s: string | Uint8Array): string => createHash("sha256").update(s).digest("hex");
    36	/** Key-sorted JSON, the form the integrity seal covers. */
    37	export function canonical(x: unknown): string {
    38	  if (Array.isArray(x)) return `[${x.map(canonical).join(",")}]`;
    39	  if (x && typeof x === "object") return `{${Object.keys(x).sort().map((k) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonical((x as Record<string, unknown>)[k])}`).join(",")}}`;
    40	  return JSON.stringify(x);
    41	}
    42	export const sealPayload = (p: Payload): Payload => { const { integritySha256: _drop, ...rest } = p; return { ...rest, integritySha256: sha256(canonical(rest)) } as Payload; };
    43	export const verifySeal = (p: Payload): boolean => { const { integritySha256, ...rest } = p; return integritySha256 === sha256(canonical(rest)); };
    44	
    45	export function generate(onProgress?: (m: string) => void): Payload {
    46	  return sealPayload(lib.buildPayload(lib.runStudy({ onProgress })));
    47	}
    48	export const serialize = (p: unknown): string => `${JSON.stringify(p, null, 1)}\n`;
    49	
    50	/**
    51	 * Files whose bytes feed the evidence: the locality library, the genotype probe and this tool. The schema and the reference
    52	 * simulator are deliberately not hashed: an unrelated edit there must not break `--check`. Changes to dynamics are caught by
    53	 * `--replay-check`, which regenerates the payload; `--check` is a no-simulation integrity check of the saved artifacts only.
    54	 */
    55	const SOURCE_DIRS = [SRC_DIR];
    56	export function sourceFiles(root = REPO, extra: readonly string[] = []): string[] {
    57	  const dirs = SOURCE_DIRS.flatMap((d) => readdirSync(`${root}${d}`).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".ts")).sort().map((f) => d + f));
    58	  return [...dirs, "packages/lineage/src/genotype.ts", "tools/locality/matrix.ts", ...extra];
    59	}
    60	export const sourceHashes = (extra: readonly string[] = []): Record<string, string> => Object.fromEntries(sourceFiles(REPO, extra).map((f) => [f, sha256(readFileSync(REPO + f))]));
    61	
    62	export function makeManifest(payload: Payload, evidenceText: string, seconds: number, extraSources: readonly string[] = [], extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Record<string, unknown> {
    63	  return {
    64	    kind: "controller-locality-manifest",
    65	    payloadFileSha256: sha256(evidenceText),
    66	    integritySha256: payload.integritySha256,
    67	    version: payload.version,
    68	    sources: sourceHashes(extraSources),
    69	    ...extra,
    70	    genomes: payload.genomes.map((g) => ({ id: g.id, hash: g.hash, cluster: g.cluster, votes: g.votes })),
    71	    candidates: payload.candidates.map((c) => ({ slot: c.slot, cluster: c.cluster, pass: c.qualification.pass, chosen: c.chosen })),
    72	    provenance: { note: "not replayed; excluded from the deterministic payload", baseCommit: BASE_COMMIT, runtime: (globalThis as { Deno?: { version: { deno: string } } }).Deno ? `deno ${(globalThis as { Deno?: { version: { deno: string } } }).Deno!.version.deno}` : `node ${process.version}`, platform: `${platform()}-${arch()}`, generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(), generateSeconds: seconds },
    73	  };
    74	}
    75	
    76	const ROW_KEYS = ["genome", "id", "family", "kind", "k", "cls", "slots", "requestedL1", "realisedL1", "undoExact", "dupOf", "hash", "expression", "changed", "maxDelta", "breadth", "participation", "sensorReach", "sensorMask", "structActuators", "structSensors", "withinStructural", "assayed", "assay"];
    77	
    78	const sameJson = (a: unknown, b: unknown): boolean => canonical(a) === canonical(b);
    79	
    80	/** Everything in the payload that follows from other saved numbers is recomputed here, so a resealed edit to a derived field is caught. */
    81	export interface CheckOptions {
    82	  /** Files beyond the shared source closure that the manifest must pin (the power tool). */
    83	  extraSources?: readonly string[];
    84	  /** The exact fixture ids a payload must carry; absent keeps PR136's behaviour (whatever it carries, each must pass). */
    85	  fixtureInventory?: readonly string[];
    86	}
    87	
    88	export function recomputeProblems(payload: Payload, opts: CheckOptions = {}): string[] {
    89	  const out: string[] = [];
    90	  const panel = payload.panel ?? lib.PANEL_V1;
    91	  for (const [i, r] of payload.rows.entries()) {
    92	    const at = `row ${i} (${r.genome} ${r.id})`;
    93	    const slotCls = r.slots.map((x) => (x.after - x.before !== x.applied ? "inconsistent" : x.applied === 0 ? "unchanged" : x.applied === x.exp ? "genuine" : "boundary-clamped"));
    94	    r.slots.forEach((x, j) => { if (x.cls !== slotCls[j]) out.push(`${at}: slot ${j} cls ${x.cls} does not follow from its before/after/expected`); });
    95	    const cls = r.slots.every((x) => x.applied === 0) ? "unchanged" : r.slots.every((x) => x.cls === "genuine") ? "genuine" : "boundary-clamped";
    96	    if (r.cls !== cls) out.push(`${at}: cls ${r.cls} does not follow from its slots (${cls})`);
    97	    if (r.k !== r.slots.length) out.push(`${at}: k ${r.k} differs from the slot count ${r.slots.length}`);
    98	    if (r.requestedL1 !== r.slots.reduce((a, x) => a + Math.abs(x.op), 0)) out.push(`${at}: requestedL1 does not follow from its slots`);
    99	    if (r.realisedL1 !== r.slots.reduce((a, x) => a + Math.abs(x.applied), 0)) out.push(`${at}: realisedL1 does not follow from its slots`);
   100	    if (r.assayed !== (r.assay !== null)) out.push(`${at}: assayed flag disagrees with the assay record`);
   101	  }
   102	  for (const [i, r] of payload.rows.entries()) {
   103	    const at = `row ${i} (${r.genome} ${r.id})`;
   104	    const permille = r.changed.map((c) => Math.floor((1000 * c) / lib.GRID_POINTS));
   105	    if (r.breadth !== permille.filter((v) => v >= lib.BREADTH_FLOOR_PERMILLE).length) out.push(`${at}: breadth does not follow from changed`);
   106	    if (r.participation !== r.maxDelta.filter((v) => v >= lib.PARTICIPATION_DELTA).length) out.push(`${at}: participation does not follow from maxDelta`);
   107	    if (r.sensorReach !== lib.popcount(r.sensorMask)) out.push(`${at}: sensorReach does not follow from sensorMask`);
   108	    let act = 0;
   109	    r.changed.forEach((c, k) => { if (c > 0) act |= 1 << k; });
   110	    if (r.withinStructural !== ((act & ~r.structActuators) === 0 && (r.sensorMask & ~r.structSensors) === 0)) out.push(`${at}: withinStructural does not follow from its masks`);
   111	    if (r.assay) {
   112	      const g = payload.genomes.find((x) => x.id === r.genome);
   113	      if (g) {
   114	        const base = [lib.sum(g.baseline.lg), lib.sum(g.baseline.dp), lib.sum(g.baseline.dt)];
   115	        if (!sameJson(r.assay.dev, r.assay.sums.map((v, k) => v - base[k]))) out.push(`${at}: assay dev does not follow from its sums and the baseline`);
   116	      }
   117	    }
   118	  }
   119	  // Sampling counts follow from the rows: population = genuine non-duplicate edits, chosen = assayed rows, per stratum.
   120	  for (const g of payload.genomes) {
   121	    const mine = payload.rows.filter((r) => r.genome === g.id);
   122	    const want: Record<string, { population: number; chosen: number }> = {};
   123	    for (const r of mine) {
   124	      const k = lib.strataKey(r as unknown as Parameters<typeof lib.strataKey>[0]);
   125	      if (r.cls === "genuine" && r.dupOf === null && !panel.exclude[g.id]?.includes(r.hash)) (want[k] ??= { population: 0, chosen: 0 }).population++;
   126	      if (r.assay) (want[k] ??= { population: 0, chosen: 0 }).chosen++;
   127	    }
   128	    const have = payload.strata[g.id] ?? {};
   129	    for (const k of new Set([...Object.keys(want), ...Object.keys(have)])) {
   130	      const w = want[k] ?? { population: 0, chosen: 0 }, h = have[k];
   131	      // Strata are recorded only for allocated keys; an unallocated stratum must hold no assayed rows.
   132	      if (!h ? w.chosen !== 0 : h.population !== w.population || h.chosen !== w.chosen) out.push(`genome ${g.id}: stratum ${k} counts do not follow from the rows`);
   133	    }
   134	    if (payload.generated) {
   135	      const gen: Record<string, number> = {};
   136	      for (const r of mine) if (r.cls === "genuine" && r.dupOf === null) { const k = lib.strataKey(r as unknown as Parameters<typeof lib.strataKey>[0]); gen[k] = (gen[k] ?? 0) + 1; }
   137	      if (!sameJson(gen, payload.generated[g.id] ?? {})) out.push(`genome ${g.id}: generated counts do not follow from the rows`);
   138	    }
   139	    const nb = payload.nullBreadth[g.id];
   140	    if (nb && nb.median2 !== lib.median2(nb.values)) out.push(`genome ${g.id}: nullBreadth median2 does not follow from its values`);
   141	    const gen0 = payload.genomes.find((x) => x.id === lib.GENERALIST_ID);
   142	    if (gen0 && !sameJson(g.tau, [lib.tau(g.baseline.lg, gen0.baseline.lg), lib.tau(g.baseline.dp, gen0.baseline.dp), lib.tau(g.baseline.dt, gen0.baseline.dt)])) out.push(`genome ${g.id}: tau does not follow from the baselines`);
   143	  }
   144	  // Selection: per slot the first passing candidate in order is used, and exactly those genomes vote.
   145	  const picked = new Set<string>();
   146	  for (const { slot } of panel.slots) {
   147	    let done = false;
   148	    for (const c of payload.candidates.filter((x) => x.slot === slot)) {
   149	      const want = c.qualification.pass && !done;
   150	      if (c.chosen !== want) out.push(`candidate ${c.id}: chosen flag does not follow from the selection rule`);
   151	      if (want) { done = true; picked.add(c.id); }
   152	    }
   153	  }
   154	  for (const g of payload.genomes) if (g.votes !== picked.has(g.id)) out.push(`genome ${g.id}: votes does not follow from candidate selection`);
   155	  if (!sameJson(payload.protocol, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(lib.protocolRecord(panel))))) out.push("protocol differs from the code's constants");
   156	  for (const f of payload.fixtures) {
   157	    const passes = f.neutral ? f.physical.identical && f.functional.changedOutputs === 0 && f.physical.dev.every((d) => d === 0) : f.functional.changedOutputs > 0;
   158	    if (f.passes !== passes) out.push(`fixture ${f.id}: passes does not follow from its numbers`);
   159	  }
   160	  if (opts.fixtureInventory) {
   161	    const have = payload.fixtures.map((f) => f.id).sort(), want = [...opts.fixtureInventory].sort();
   162	    if (!sameJson(have, want)) out.push(`fixture inventory differs from the expected one (have ${have.join(", ")})`);
   163	    if (!payload.fixtures.some((f) => f.neutral)) out.push("no neutral fixture is present");
   164	  }
   165	  const neutralOk = payload.fixtures.filter((f) => f.neutral).every((f) => f.passes);
   166	  if (payload.neutralControlsOk !== neutralOk) out.push("neutralControlsOk does not follow from the fixtures");
   167	  const gen = payload.genomes.find((g) => g.id === lib.GENERALIST_ID);
   168	  const sensPasses = gen !== undefined && payload.sensitivity.dev < -payload.sensitivity.tau;
   169	  if (payload.sensitivity.passes !== sensPasses) out.push("sensitivity.passes does not follow from its dev and tau");
   170	  if (gen && payload.sensitivity.tau !== gen.tau[0]) out.push("sensitivity.tau differs from the generalist light-growth tau");
   171	  for (const g of payload.genomes) {
   172	    const q = lib.qualify(g.baseline);
   173	    if (!sameJson(q, g.qualification)) out.push(`genome ${g.id}: qualification does not follow from its baseline`);
   174	  }
   175	  for (const c of payload.candidates) if (!sameJson(lib.qualify(c.baseline), c.qualification)) out.push(`candidate ${c.id}: qualification does not follow from its baseline`);
   176	  for (const g of payload.genomes) {
   177	    const want = lib.readoutFor(g, payload.rows, payload.strata[g.id] ?? {}, payload.nullBreadth[g.id]?.median2 ?? 0);
   178	    const have = payload.readouts.find((r) => r.genome === g.id);
   179	    if (!have || !sameJson(have, want)) out.push(`genome ${g.id}: saved readout does not follow from the saved rows`);
   180	  }
   181	  if (payload.readouts.length !== payload.genomes.length) out.push("readouts and genomes differ in number");
   182	  return out;
   183	}
   184	
   185	/** Validate saved artifacts without simulating. Returns problems (empty when sound). */
   186	export function checkSaved(evidencePath = EVIDENCE, manifestPath = MANIFEST, root = REPO, opts: CheckOptions = {}): string[] {
   187	  const problems: string[] = [];
   188	  if (!existsSync(evidencePath) || !existsSync(manifestPath)) return ["evidence.json or manifest.json is missing"];
   189	  const text = readFileSync(evidencePath, "utf8");
   190	  const payload = JSON.parse(text) as Payload;
   191	  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, "utf8"));
   192	  if (!verifySeal(payload)) problems.push("integritySha256 does not match the payload");
   193	  if (manifest.payloadFileSha256 !== sha256(text)) problems.push("manifest payloadFileSha256 does not match evidence.json");
   194	  if (manifest.integritySha256 !== payload.integritySha256) problems.push("manifest seal differs from the payload seal");
   195	  const ids = new Set(payload.genomes.map((g) => g.id));
   196	  for (const g of manifest.genomes as { id: string; hash: string }[]) if (payload.genomes.find((x) => x.id === g.id)?.hash !== g.hash) problems.push(`genome ${g.id}: manifest hash differs`);
   197	  let rowsWellFormed = true;
   198	  for (const [i, r] of payload.rows.entries()) {
   199	    const missing = ROW_KEYS.filter((k) => !(k in r));
   200	    if (missing.length) { problems.push(`row ${i} lacks ${missing.join(", ")}`); rowsWellFormed = false; }
   201	    if (!ids.has(r.genome)) problems.push(`row ${i} names unknown genome ${r.genome}`);
   202	  }
   203	  if (payload.grid.points !== lib.GRID_POINTS) problems.push(`grid points ${payload.grid.points} differ from the derived ${lib.GRID_POINTS}`);
   204	  try { lib.assertIntegers(payload); } catch (e) { problems.push(String((e as Error).message)); }
   205	  if (rowsWellFormed) {
   206	    problems.push(...recomputeProblems(payload, opts));
   207	    const again = lib.verdictFromPayload(payload);
   208	    if (canonical(again) !== canonical(payload.verdict)) problems.push("the saved verdict does not follow from the saved rows");
   209	  }
   210	  if (!payload.neutralControlsOk) problems.push("a neutral control was not neutral");
   211	  if (existsSync(root + SRC_DIR)) for (const f of sourceFiles(root, opts.extraSources)) if (!Object.keys(manifest.sources).includes(f)) problems.push(`source ${f} is not in the manifest (added since the evidence was written)`);
   212	  for (const [file, hash] of Object.entries(manifest.sources as Record<string, string>)) {
   213	    if (!existsSync(root + file)) problems.push(`source ${file} is missing`);
   214	    else if (sha256(readFileSync(root + file)) !== hash) problems.push(`source ${file} changed since the evidence was written`);
   215	  }
   216	  return problems;
   217	}
   218	
   219	export function replayCheck(evidencePath = EVIDENCE): string[] {
   220	  return serialize(generate()) === readFileSync(evidencePath, "utf8") ? [] : ["regenerated payload differs from evidence.json"];
   221	}
   222	
   223	function calibrate(): void {
   224	  const t0 = performance.now();
   225	  const gen = lib.generalistStart();
   226	  const cache = new lib.AssayCache();
   227	  const timeRun = (w: Uint32Array, sc: import("@bl/lineage").Scenario) => { const t = performance.now(); const r = lib.runAssay(w, sc, 1); return [r.permille, Math.round(performance.now() - t)]; };
   228	  console.log("scenario protocol", JSON.stringify(lib.ASSAY));
   229	  for (const s of [gen, ...[4, 10, 16, 9].map((c) => lib.m3Start(c, c === 4 || c === 10 ? "m3-sparse" : "m3-dense"))]) {
   230	    const b = { lg: lib.responses(cache, s.words, "light-growth"), dp: lib.responses(cache, s.words, "dark-persistence"), dt: lib.responses(cache, s.words, "dim-transfer") };
   231	    console.log(s.id, "baseline", JSON.stringify(b), "gate", JSON.stringify(lib.qualify(b)), "ms(lg,dp,dt seed1)", JSON.stringify(lib.SCENARIO_IDS.map((sc) => timeRun(s.words, sc))));
   232	  }
   233	  console.log(`calibrate took ${Math.round(performance.now() - t0)} ms; budget: assayed edits per M3 genome ${Object.values(lib.ALLOC_M3).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)}, scenarios 3, seeds ${lib.ASSAY.seeds.length}, horizon ${lib.ASSAY.horizon}`);
   234	}
   235	
   236	async function main(): Promise<void> {
   237	  const mode = process.argv[2];
   238	  if (mode === "--write") {
   239	    if (existsSync(EVIDENCE) && !process.argv.includes("--force")) throw new Error("evidence.json exists; files are written once. Pass --force to rewrite deliberately.");
   240	    const t = performance.now();
   241	    const payload = generate((m) => console.error(m));
   242	    const text = serialize(payload);
   243	    const manifestText = serialize(makeManifest(payload, text, Math.round((performance.now() - t) / 1000)));
   244	    writeFileSync(`${EVIDENCE}.tmp`, text);
   245	    writeFileSync(`${MANIFEST}.tmp`, manifestText);
   246	    renameSync(`${EVIDENCE}.tmp`, EVIDENCE);
   247	    renameSync(`${MANIFEST}.tmp`, MANIFEST);
   248	    console.log(`wrote evidence.json (${text.length} bytes) seal ${payload.integritySha256}`);
   249	  } else if (mode === "--check" || mode === "--replay-check") {
   250	    const problems = mode === "--check" ? checkSaved() : replayCheck();
   251	    if (problems.length) { console.error(problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   252	    console.log(`${mode} ok`);
   253	  } else if (mode === "--calibrate") calibrate();
   254	  else {
   255	    console.error("usage: deno run -A --no-lock tools/locality/matrix.ts --write | --check | --replay-check | --calibrate");
   256	    process.exit(2);
   257	  }
   258	}
   259	const isMain = (import.meta as { main?: boolean }).main ?? (process.argv[1] !== undefined && pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href === import.meta.url);
   260	if (isMain) await main();
